# Revisão das missões, uma a uma

Revisão de leitura (agentes Haiku 5.5, 2026-10-17) das 339 missões de `QuestTemps`: aceitar (5012), cumprir
(`QuestCondictionInfos`), guia do cliente e entregar (5014). Retrato do código em `milestone-1` antes da leva de
correções das missões; o estado atual de cada tipo de condição fica em [missoes.md](missoes.md).

Resultado (milestone-1): **18 ok**, **34 parcial**, **287 trava**. Com a cidade externa: **18 ok**, **36 parcial**, **285 trava**.

Atualização (cidade-externa, 2026-10-17): **100** e **411** passam de trava para parcial (o tipo 8 agora conta a vitória contra o castelo-robô; a cadeia ainda trava). **765** segue trava: exige guilda (IsLeague) e não re-aceita a diária. **90** segue trava: o 2100001 vem de batalha de campanha, não de drop. Na cidade externa, os robôs ficaram só na instância do castelo. Nenhuma chegou a ok. Coleta (55, 102, 140, 170, 171, 172): o 8258 com a 6001 segue recusado, com aviso de indisponível; o `.maps` da 6001 existe no CDN local, mas sem nó de campanha, e não há captura de 8258 com a 6001 nem de 5032 em nó de coleta (ver [cidade-externa.md](cidade-externa.md)).

## Problemas que se repetem

- Legenda de caminhos: servidor = D:\wartune\servidor\src\wartune_server\game\ (rules.py, outer_city.py, castle.py, stars.py, tower.py, arena.py via handlers\ quando indicado) e D:\wartune\servidor\src\wartune_server\content\tutorial.json; tabelas = D:\wartune\servidor\data\overlay\xml\tempinfos.xml; cliente = D:\wartune\decompiled\m2\scripts\yishi\manager\TaskManage.as e ...\mvc\module\task\TaskTemplate.as; guia = D:\wartune\decompiled\m8\scripts\mediators\NewbieBaseConditionMediator.as e NewbieBaseActionMediator.as; captura = D:\wartune\server-relay\replay_packets.json (idx = índice do pacote).
- Batalhas de campanha só iniciam para as chaves de tutorial.json['battles'] (campaign.py:258: 10001, 10002, 1000103, 1000104, 1000106, 1000203, 1000205, 8251). Qualquer outro nó (Kurt İni 1011xx e 1021xx, Vahşiler 1071-1083, etc.) não tem batalha no servidor. Isso bloqueia as missões 21, 70, 80 e a cadeia 90/100 e o guia 25500.
- rules.py:190, :297, :314 e :325 leem só a primeira linha de QuestCondictionInfos (templates.get pega a primeira). Missões com várias linhas ignoram as outras: 8 e 13 têm 4 cada; 240, 450, 680, 730 e 764 estão fora da lista. Corrigir com templates.find('QuestCondictionInfos', TemplateId=...) e exigir todas.
- Entrega antes do progresso: handlers/player.py:81-82 recusa e esquece o 5014. A captura mostra o oficial concluindo sozinho quando o progresso chega: 70 (5014 idx 1171 -> concluída idx 1245), 80 (idx 1661 -> 1750), 82 (idx 1194 -> 1305). Implementar 'entrega pendente' (flag na Quest) e concluir nos caminhos de progresso (quest_event, update_building_quests, update_novice_quests).
- Tipos sem nenhum progresso no servidor: 1, 14, 20, 26, 27, 28, 32, 33, 35, 36, 38, 39, 45, 49, 50. quest_event só é chamado para os tipos 3 (army.py:92), 10 (army.py:178), 52 (army.py:247), 34 (army.py:286), 53 (stars.py:316) e 7 (outer_city.py:266); os tipos 2 e 23 têm caminhos próprios (update_building_quests, update_novice_quests). Afetam 23 das 34 missões: 19 vencidas e 4 vivas (3, 6, 70, 80).
- Completude decidida pelo cliente em vários tipos: checkGoodsCount 27/28/32/49 (TaskTemplate.as:789), checkClientLogin 33 (:821), checkSencondLogin 39 (:698), checkPlayerGrades 1 (:869), checkMazeRecord 38 (:753, com maxIndex fixo 60 vindo de tower.py:29). O cliente marca completo, o 5014 é recusado em silêncio (player.py:82) e o jogador fica sem saída. Fix: resposta de erro ao 5014 recusado e progresso autoritativo no servidor. isCompleted (TaskTemplate.as:330-348) é derivado das strings de progresso.
- accept_quest (rules.py:280-288; player.py:65-73) não valida NeedMinLevel/NeedMaxLevel, PreQuestId, StartDate/EndDate nem IsRepeat/RepeatInterval/RepeatMax; confia no cliente (TaskManage.isAvailableTask, 274-338). Por isso aceite fora de nível ou antes do pré-requisito passa (ex.: Q82 aceita no nível 1). A captura mostra o oficial aceitando a 80 (5012 idx 1172) antes da 70 fechar (idx 1245). IsAuto=true nas 34 (TaskManage.as:498 e :512): aceite automático pelo cliente.
- Missões repetíveis (IsRepeat=true: 1, 4, 8, 9, 13, 14, 17, 18, 19, 22): accept_quest recusa se o registro já existe (rules.py:282), mesmo concluído, então nunca repetem. Hoje todas estão vencidas, sem efeito prático.
- Recompensas ignoradas em quest_finish (player.py:86-89 lê só RewardPlayGP e RewardGold): RewardStrategy (16: 50000; 17: 5000; 19: 10000), RewardCrystal (15: 5000), RewardMoney (1: 50), RewardPlayerOffer (1: 10). IsSelect é ignorado (rewardItemId não é lido), mas nenhuma das 34 tem IsSelect=true. ValidDate é ignorado (todos 0).
- free_bag_pos (rules.py:400-402) e quest_finish (player.py:90-92) não checam o tamanho da bolsa: os itens de missão entram mesmo com a bolsa cheia.
- campaign.py:505 aceita qualquer noviceProcess enviado pelo cliente no 5017: isso destrava as missões tipo 23 (10, 20, 30, 40, 45, 50) sem passar pelo tutorial. Limitar a valores que avancem a partir do atual.
- Datas: 20 das 34 missões têm EndDate passado (2000-01-01 em 5, 7, 20, 21, 22, 55; 2000-10-12 em 2 e 4; 2011-2012 nas demais). checkIsAchieved (TaskManage.as:340-349) as oculta, e a captura oficial não aceitou nenhuma delas. Vivas: 3, 6, 10, 30, 40, 45, 50, 51, 52, 60, 70, 80, 81, 82 (14 missões). Vencidas, com dado morto nos dois lados: 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 11-22, 55 e 20.

- Aceitar sem validação (5012, D:/wartune/servidor/src/wartune_server/game/handlers/player.py:65-73; rules.py:280-288): não confere PreQuestId, nível, data, repetição nem tipo. A única barreira é o cliente (TaskManage.isAvailableTask, 274-338). Um cliente adulterado aceita qualquer missão ativa nos dados. Confiar no cliente é um trade-off a registrar.
- Cobertura de condições: quest_event é chamado só em seis pontos (army.py:92 tipo 3, army.py:178 tipo 10, army.py:247 tipo 52, army.py:286 tipo 34, outer_city.py:266 tipo 7 (padrão que funciona, missão 60), stars.py:316 LOCK_QUEST fora desta lista). Entre as 34 missões, só 187 (tipo 23), 181 e 182 (tipo 2), 110 (tipo 3), 101 (tipo 10) e 160 (tipo 34) avançam.
- Progresso por evento x estado: 21, 22, 27, 32, 33 e 37 são estado no cliente. Mesmo com hook de evento, a missão trava se o estado já existia antes de aceitar (amigo já adicionado, aliança já criada, item já na bolsa, castelo já no mapa 1). A correção comum é calcular o progresso pelo estado em accept_quest e quest_done (rules.py:280-326), como update_building_quests já faz com prédios. Para 33 e 85 o servidor não recebe sinal nenhum (só o cliente sabe). Para 34 o servidor recebe o sinal, mas o cliente perde o flag isSorted no relogin.

Não paga RewardStrategy (Ruh: 90 com 1000, 101 e 160 com 2000, 185 com 80000), RewardCrystal (170 com 500), RewardHeroGP, RewardDower, RewardMoney, RewardConsortiaOffer nem RewardPlayerOffer. - Outside-set gate chain blocks the Meclis ladder: 173 (t37, Özgürlük Otlağı) <- 210 (t26 kill Fhyrona 1031) <- 190 (t27 collect item 2100003 x5) <- 265 (Kışla 10, t2) <- 282 (t7 OCCUPY, hooked) <- 312 (Depo 12, t2) <- 351 (Meclis 15, t2) <- 391. checkPreTask (TaskManage.as:406-423) requires IsTaskFinish (bitmap, 477-491), so 391, 412, 441, 461, 472, 502 and 505 (via 780 ... 551 <- 502) are never offered. Making 173, 210 and 190 completable is the prerequisite for 7 of the 34.
- Other outside gates: 350 (t14 group scene 9000) blocks 370 and 371; 360 <- 310 (t26 kill Syrmon 1071) <- 300 (t28 item 2100005) blocks 380 and 390; 921 (t26 Peri campaign 20010) <- 920 (t28 item 2100025) <- 922 (t85 Anna talk, no hook) blocks 504 even if its own hook existed.
- Item-collect (t27/t28) has no progress path (quest_done rules.py:324-326 checks progress only). Items 2100007-2100013 are absent from every drop, shop, craft and quest row (tempinfos parse). A scan of 9,366 project files finds them only in tempinfos.xml and its copies under .claude/worktrees; shop.py and docs/itens.md mention only 208001. They are absent from the decompiled client and from all decoded captures (only 208001 appears, in 115 and 4475 messages). Affects 370, 400, 420, 460, 500, 520, 540. A data-owner source is needed before any server fix helps.
- Only the first QuestCondictionInfos row counts (rules.py:190 quest_event, rules.py:325 quest_done). builders/player.py:20-22 send condition_2..4 = 0. Quest 450 can never complete on the client (TaskTemplate.as:330-348 checks all rows), and fixing it needs per-row progress (single column, schema.py:334).
- quest_finish (handlers/player.py:86-89) pays only RewardPlayGP and RewardGold, plus QuestGoods (90-92). Never paid: RewardCrystal (371: 1000; 505: 100000), RewardConsortiaOffer (422: 40) and RewardPlayerOffer (422: 20). RewardHeroGP, RewardDower, RewardMoney and RewardStrategy are 0 in all 34 rows.
- Repeatable quests 371 and 422 can run only once. accept_quest refuses any existing row (rules.py:282; DB uq_quests_character_id_template_id at schema.py:337 and migration 0001_baseline.py:200). handlers/login.py:214 drops completed quests from 112, and builders/player.py:24 hard-codes repeatFinish=1, so the client has no taskInfo for TaskManage.as:380-404. Official login.json still sends completed 371 with completedDate and repeatFinish.
- accept_quest (rules.py:280-288) checks only that the template exists and has no row. It does not enforce PreQuestId, NeedMinLevel/NeedMaxLevel, StartDate/EndDate or NeedBuildingTemp; the client (TaskManage.as:274-338) is the only gate. A crafted 5012 can accept expired 540 or skip prerequisites (for example 530 before 370).
- quest_done (rules.py:325-326) returns True when a quest has no condition row, so 5014 completes it with zero progress (fail-open). No quest in the 34 is affected, since all have rows.
- Silent refusal: handlers/player.py:81-83 logs and returns with no reply on a refused 5014. For t27/t28, t2 and t37 the client already shows the quest complete (client-side counting), so the player clicks and nothing happens (mission-60 class).
- Official server auto-completes event quests without 5014: peri_turnuvasi.json shows 112 condition_1=1 isComplete=true, then 52 for 504, and no 5014. Ours completes only on 5014 (handlers/player.py:76-109). Setting progress at the join is enough for the client click path, but it does not match the official auto-complete.
- Client navigation gaps (guide, not server): gotoPvpFrame needs grades >= 22 (SwitchPageHelp 1313-1340) while 421 and 422 start at 18; gotoHeroDoor needs newbieProcess > 39000 (650-677), used by 371; type 37 (390, 173) only opens the task frame; the type 8 nav is gotoOuterCity.
- Newbie guide: none of the 752 nodes in newbie.xml waits on the 34 IDs (checked c4/c12/c22 guards and all param tokens). The hard-coded quest 60 in NewbieBaseConditionMediator.as:195-209 is the same bug class, outside this set.
- Walk targets are valid: campaigns 1101-1107 and 9000-9002 exist; map 10000 node 3 (Anna, Peri Turnuvası, SonType 3001) and node 15 (Ametist Madeni, Transfer to map 30000) exist (MapNodeInfos key Id).
- Checked OK: STARTING_BUILDINGS (characters.py:77-84) includes 50101 and 40201, so 471's PreTemplateId holds at start. Completion reaches the client through quest_bitmap (rules.py:267-273), and QUEST_SITE_LEN=200 bytes (builders/login.py:23) covers every ID.
- Not verified: job_type mapping (rules.py:29) against ToJob 1/2/3 for 530; the tournament handler (no name match for tourn\|peri in game/); the PvP attack hook point for 411; the item source for 190's 2100003 and for gate items 2100005, 2100006 and 2100025.
- Legenda: S = D:/wartune/servidor/src/wartune_server; C = D:/wartune/decompiled/m2/scripts; M8 = D:/wartune/decompiled/m8/scripts. Definições: trava = a missão não pode ser concluída no servidor hoje (falta gancho, batalha, fonte de item ou dado, ou o cliente conclui e o servidor recusa). parcial = o mecanismo próprio está correto (gancho, 112, 5014 e recompensa), mas um pré-requisito está em trava, então ninguém chega a ela. ok = nenhuma das 34. Contagem: 0 ok, 9 parcial (551, 581, 621, 623, 681, 711, 721, 731, 751), 25 trava. Nenhuma das 34 tem congelamento com evidência; os casos de entrada em campanha ficam como não verificado.

- PROCESSO (regra SÓ LEITURA). Minhas execuções anteriores de Python com -I (que ignora PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE; só as últimas usaram -B) provavelmente gravaram bytecode em D:/wartune/servidor/src/wartune_server (há 115 .pyc com data de hoje e 16 pastas __pycache__ em src). Não apaguei nada, porque não dá para separar dos caches que já existiam. Se quiserem limpar, apagar os .pyc com data de hoje é seguro. Nenhum código, commit ou download.
- Totals: 34 quests exist in 832-866 and all 34 are trava; 834 has no row and is ok. The cause is mostly missing server hooks and missing campaign or farm features, not data errors (items 1-3).
- Path shorthand: server files are under D:/wartune/servidor/src/wartune_server/ (game/rules.py, game/handlers/*.py, game/builders/player.py, domain/models.py, persistence/migrations/versions/0001_baseline.py). Client classes are under D:/wartune/decompiled/m2/scripts/ (yishi/manager/TaskManage.as, mvc/module/task/TaskTemplate.as, mvc/module/task/TaskCondictionTemplate.as, yishi/SwitchPageHelp.as, mvc/controler/SelectCampaignController.as, farm/FarmManager.as, farm/view/FarmLandItem.as, yishi/manager/BaseManager.as, yishi/manager/RoomListSocketOutManager.as). - 1. Hook coverage: the server advances quest progress only for kinds 2, 3, 7, 10, 23, 34, 52 and 53. Callers: army.py:92/178/247/286 (kinds 3, 10, 52, 34), outer_city.py:266 (7), stars.py:316 (53), castle.py:115 (kind 2 via update_building_quests), campaign.py:506 and accept_quest rules.py:286-287 (kind 23). Kinds 1, 14, 25, 26, 49 and 57-63 have no caller. In QuestCondictionInfos they appear 30 (kind 1), 39 (14), 38 (25), 30 (26), 15 (49) and about 11 (57-63) times, so the same trava affects many quests outside 832-866.
- 2. Battles: the only campaign battle starts are tutorial ones. campaign.py:258-259 (arrive 5125) and 279-280 (callback 5032) call _start_battle (379-392), which reads tutorial()['battles']; special_boss (395-412, BOSS 8251) is the tutorial boss. The other Battle( constructions are arena.py:230/484 and outer_city.py:239 (mine occupation). No trial, group or dungeon battle can start. Node data is missing as well: CampaignData has 130 rows, all for campaign ids 40001 (100) and 40002 (30), which are not in Campaigns; MapNodeInfos has 70 rows, all on map 10000 (Gökyüzü). Dungeons 1001-1007 have no CampaignData. Kinds 14, 25 and 26 need a battle system plus captured node data before any hook can fire.
- 3. campaign_map.py documents its own gaps: 5180 campaign finish (lines 67-70) is a no-op ('progresso de campanha não modelado'); 5121 (49-52), 5122 (55-58) and 5033 (27-31) have no effect; 5126 fog is not stored (61-64); 5038 gives a preload list only for maps 1011, 1021 and 2011 (SYSTEM_ARMY, 16-24).
- 4. Entry and freeze risk. Solo trials: gotoCampaignById (SwitchPageHelp.as:833-901) checks Capacity 1 and MinLevel (880-884), then opens the swf-only selectcampaign frame (899). SelectCampaignController.as:65-74 preloads swf/singlecampaign1/<chapter>/map/<chapter>.swf. The frame's enter request is not in the decompiled scripts, so its opcode is unverified. The room opcodes the scripts send (5023 create at RoomListSocketOutManager.as:64-77; 5024 enter at :45-52) carry no campaign id, and room.py:127-157 ignores any. room.py sends only S_ROOM. S_ROOM_CREATED with a campaignId is sent only by _enter_map (campaign.py:199-201) and enter_pet_island (space.py:91-93). The only client send of 5127 in the decompiled scripts is at login for newbieProcess <= 18500 (BaseManager.as:1147-1152). So a trial entry gets no trial map and no battle; whether the frame masks the screen while it waits is unverified. Group: Capacity 4 needs newbieProcess > 39000 (SwitchPageHelp.as:868-875), then goSpaceAndFind(2) (:876; defined at 1640-1658) sets spaceNodeId and walks to node 2, the Kahraman Sarayı NPC (MapNodeInfos map 10000 Id 2, MasterType 61). Clicking it gets an S_EVENT reply (space.py:58-68), so the NPC UI opens. The group dungeon then has no start (room.py has no start handler; item 2).
- 5. Client completes, server refuses, no freeze: kinds 1 (TaskTemplate.as:869-876 checkPlayerGrades) and 49 (TaskTemplate.as:789-797 checkGoodsCount) show complete locally. The claim (5014) is refused with no reply (handlers/player.py:81-83, log '5014 recusado'). TaskFrameII.as (m15 :569-594) sends the claim and returns with no lock. FarmManager.sendFarmOper (FarmManager.as:542-557) sets only payType. CompleteTaskAction dismisses after its timeout (earlier read). The UI does not freeze; the reward just never arrives.
- 6. Repeat quests can never repeat on the server: accept_quest refuses any existing row (rules.py:282); the table has a unique (character_id, template_id) constraint (0001_baseline.py:200); quest_info always sends repeatFinish=1 (builders/player.py:24). The client gates checkRepeatMax and checkInRepeatTime (TaskManage.as:316, 380-404) let the quest through on the next day, so it sends 5012 again and gets no reply (handlers/player.py:70-73). Affects 846-856 and 863 (daily, RepeatInterval 24). The client's day boundary is UTC-5 (TaskManage.as:394-396).
- 7. Trust boundary: accept_quest (rules.py:280-288) checks only that the template exists and is not already accepted. It does not check PreQuestId, NeedMinLevel/NeedMaxLevel, dates, IsLeague, WeekSpace or HourSpace. A crafted 5012 can therefore accept any quest (for example 833 without 832). The client gates are TaskManage.as:274-338.
- 8. Expired quests: 842, 844 and 857 have EndDate 2000-01-01. The client hides them (TaskManage.as:340-349 checkIsAchieved) and the server never checks dates, so they are dead data. Re-enabling them needs the EndDate fixed too.
- 9. Rewards not paid: quest_finish (handlers/player.py:85-89) pays RewardPlayGP, RewardGold and QuestGoods (ToJob filter at rules.py:337). Not paid: RewardCrystal (832 20000, 843 25000, 845 25000, 857 20000) and RewardConsortiaOffer/RewardPlayerOffer (846-856, 20 each). The fields exist: models.py:102 crystals, 131-132 consortia_offer and consortia_total_offer, 642-643 offer and total_offer (confirm the owning class). RewardStrategy, RewardHeroGP, RewardDower and RewardMoney are empty in range.
- 10. Koyu Kristal (208012) has four data references and no server code reference. FishTemplateInfos 1004 RewardId: fishing is closed (minigames.py:288-292 returns an empty pool). ShopTempInfos 755 (ShopType 7, Score 18) and 810 (ShopType 10, LordsScore 1): both unbuyable, because PRICE_COLUMNS (shop.py:36) lacks Score and LordsScore and price() returns None (shop.py:42-46). QuestCondictionInfos 846-856. The item text (ItemTempInfos 208012) says it is used for the daily guild quest, so the intended source is the guild extra scene, which has no server handler (earlier search).
- 11. The labor ratio (kind 63, quest 863) has no action in the server source or protocol; grep for labor, emek and ratio found nothing. This is a missing feature, not only a missing hook.
- 12. Comma Para1 (latent crash): 17 QuestCondictionInfos rows have a comma list in Para1, all kind 14 or 26: 760, 783, 784, 842, 843, 844, 845, 893, 894, 895, 896, 901, 902, 903, 904, 923, 924 (for example 842 '3062,4062' and 843 '9006,9106'). quest_event runs int(cond['Para1']) at rules.py:193 for every accepted quest of the hooked kind, so the first hook for kind 14 or 26 without a comma split crashes for any player holding such a quest. quest_event also ignores Para3 (rules.py:185-199), so kill and boss hooks must filter by the campaign in Para3.
- 13. Multi-row conditions: TemplateIds 8, 13, 240, 450, 680, 730 and 764 have more than one QuestCondictionInfos row, and only the first is read (templates.get in rules.py:190, 297, 314, 325). Outside the range.

- 16. Level gate: NeedMinLevel is 1 for 832-841, but the solo trials need MinLevel 50-58 (Campaigns: 1401 50, 1402 52, 1403 54, 1404 54, 1405 56, 1406 56, 1407 58). gotoCampaignById refuses with a tip below MinLevel (SwitchPageHelp.as:880-884). The chain can be accepted at level 1 but not completed until about level 50-58. Confirm that this is intended.
- 17. Farm: farm_operate refuses all friend operations (farm.py:64-66) and has no handler for opType 5, the client's friend steal (FarmLandItem.as:183). The server constants are REQ 0, PLANT 1, WORM 2, WEED 3, PICK 4, ACCELERATE 7, CLEAR 8 (farm.py:25). The ConfigInfos Farm_TodayStolen_Count and Farm_TodayFromFriend_GP (200 each) are never read. farm.py:68 routes both WORM and WEED to _pest, so the pest hook must count WORM only.
- 18. Item stacks: 208014 (858, x100) and 208015 (863, x10) have MaxCount 9999, so the counts are safe. quest_finish appends each reward as a new stack at a free position (handlers/player.py:90-92) and never merges with an existing stack or checks MaxCount. This is minor and outside the range.

No repo files were changed, nothing was committed and nothing was downloaded. A entrada na ilha (space.py:100-107 e nos de transferencia) nao checa a 920, o que e divergencia.
- Progresso de campanha e batalha: campaign progress nao e modelado (game/handlers/campaign_map.py:69-70; dungeon.py:17-18), entao o tipo 14 nao tem como validar. A batalha nao emite vitoria para missoes (game/battle.py:245-262, vencedores em 252; game/handlers/battle.py sem vitoria). As cenas 9009, 9010, 9011, 9012, 9110, 3001 e 8101 existem em Campaigns, mas nao tem CampaignData nem .maps no repo (so 3 .maps em D:\\wartune: 100, 10001, 10002). Conteudo de mapa nao verificavel aqui; isso nao prova ausencia.
- Sistemas que as missoes pedem e estao fechados ou ausentes: veiculo (game/handlers/vehicle.py:1-5 e 48-52; 8608 sem resposta; missoes 912-915), caca ao tesouro (minigames.py:279-285; 930), coleta sem 143 (space.py:141-144; 927-929), ilha dos pets (space.py:82-86; 920-921), criacao de cristal (926, nao localizada).
- Guias do cliente (mesma classe do bug da missao 60): (1) sala de veiculo espera 8608/8609 que nao chegam (SwitchPageHelp.as:318-321); (2) tela de caca ao tesouro abre e o servidor responde fechada (SwitchPageHelp.as:328-330 x minigames.py:279-285); (3) portao do tesouro espera IsTaskFinish(933) (DialogOptionItem.as:208-217); (4) guarda de pets espera a 920 (ArmyFrameII.as:672); (5) setas cross-map para 920/921 ('20001,2000104', '20001,2000149'), 922 ('10000,3'), 932 ('10000,20') e 933 ('10000,19') (SwitchPageHelp.as:85-89): todos os nos existem (content/petisland.json mapa 20001; MapNodeInfos MapId 10000 Id 3, 19 e 20). Setas OK.
- Guia m8: nenhuma dependencia encontrada. Grep de literais 890-939 em todo m8 so acha coordenadas (NewbieSpecialActionMediator.as:83,132 com 520,920). GuideValueTemps Id 222 e tipo 5 = USE_STAR (GuideTaskType.as:14), com id de estrela 904, nao missao. Ressalva: os passos do guia nao foram lidos; checkTaskIsComplete/checkTaskHasCompleted (NewbieBaseConditionMediator.as:96-110) recebem id de dado (NewbieConditionType.as:12,28).
- Dados: 911 tem Para2 150 (tipo 78), inalcançavel (RuneGrade maximo 10; RUNE_MAX_GRADE hero.py:24; ThaneInfoHelper.as:402). Corrigir no overlay (a descricao diz nivel 3). QuestCondictionInfos tem 352 linhas para 338 missoes, e GameTemplates.get pega so a primeira linha (templates/repository.py:110-118; rules.py:190,297,314,325). As 32 tem 1 linha cada, mas outras missoes podem ter mais. quest_done retorna True se nao houver linha de condicao (rules.py:326), entao essa missao e entregue sem checar.
- Capturas: login.json (indice 89, S>C 112) traz 915 e 933 aceitas com condition_1=0, isComplete false e repeatFinish 1, o que bate com builders/player.py:15-28. Varredura completa de captures/*.json e server-relay/replay_packets.json (decoder do projeto): nenhum 5012, 5014 ou 52 para 901-933. Nao ha prova oficial de como o 5014 tratava estas missoes.
- Limites: nao li os passos do guia m8 (ver item do guia m8); nao verifiquei o conteudo das cenas (sem .maps local); fonte dos itens 2100025 e 2100031 nao confirmada; o veredito parcial dos tipos 85 pressupoe que o servidor confie no cliente (ponytail).
